# LIMS modernization — phased implementation plan

Status: Phase 1 is complete with verified publication. Remote main contains Claude Code's Phase 2 "Plano" implementation and Phase 3 journey work; their visual/release acceptance remains as recorded in those sections. Phase 4 is implemented with focused verification and Phase 5 is functionally complete under its frozen closure gates. The user authorized integrating both histories, validating the combined state and publishing it to main. Both histories are integrated and verified for normal publication; no production deployment or new Phase 6 work is authorized. Updated 2026-10-04.

## Authorized main integration checkpoint — 2026-10-04

The integration preserves local checkpoint `913a0557c256c6e0f228d7f7c841d6e9fadc0960` and remote main `e970acd48716f0ab2548c01815f9a6bdf1a83561` as separate parents of a merge, without rewriting either history. Plano's shell, typography and flat components remain; the local receiving permissions, recoverable procurement actions, guarded report downloads, classified stock movements, grouped network indicators and portal resource-aware identity remain intact. Generated builds and new verification screenshots stay on disk but untracked. Existing design-reference artifacts are preserved.

Three direct certificate-release regressions first reproduced missing owning-lab membership/verification checks and a save veto that still created success evidence. Release now uses the existing transactional mutation-access service and rejects failed persistence before signature/audit creation. This is a bounded integration repair, not a new scientific policy or module audit. The imported end-to-end journey passes, including cross-lab isolation and signed release replay. Browser-discovered overflow of a long financial document identifier is fixed by wrapping it; issued-core restrictions are unchanged.

| Check | Combined candidate evidence |
| --- | --- |
| PHP regression | **1,131 cases / 9,476 assertions**, 31 named files, **679.74 seconds**. Manifest identities reconcile exactly with JUnit: no missing, extra or duplicate cases; no failures, errors or skips. Includes the frozen Phase 5 closure files, new journey/release cases, scientific mutation/concurrency, intake/correction, proposal authoring, inventory/report boundaries, network access and smoke checks. Isolated concurrency fixtures replay all migrations in disposable test schemas. |
| Frontend | All **531 checks** pass after aligning stale shell/style contracts with Plano while retaining behavioral assertions. |
| Build and source | Production build passes in **23.06 seconds**; all **630 referenced files** exist. Existing large-chunk warnings remain. Pint and staged whitespace checks pass; no conflict markers or unresolved Git entries remain. |
| Browser | Existing fictional staff and portal accounts used without password resets or permission grants. Plano desktop shell loads at 1512 px. The financial observation editor and portal fit 320 px without document overflow; both mobile drawers open/close, and portal Escape restores focus to its navigation trigger. Mobile staff drawer screenshot inspected. |

Publication is a normal push after this checkpoint; its exact remote SHA is verified and reported in the conversation. This does not claim full screenshot/spec matching, complete visual acceptance, the complete PHP release suite, production migration execution or deployment. The known unavailable local realtime websocket and Phase 6 release holds remain unchanged. Earlier phase notes below are historical evidence, not newly reopened work.

## Authoritative Phase 1 acceptance contract — frozen 2026-10-03

This contract supersedes every historical open-ended audit, suggested next batch and instruction to begin Phase 2 in this document. Completed evidence and existing changes are preserved, not discarded. Only reproducible evidence of a blocker to one of these gates permits additional Phase 1 work; record the evidence and a named acceptance test here. Do not reopen a completed gate without a relevant source change or contradictory evidence. No interface redesign, screenshot matching or Phase 2 preparation is authorized. Nonessential migration consolidation and supporting-module redesign are deferred.

| Gate | State | Acceptance and evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. Freeze scope and decisions | PASS | This section limits work to PostgreSQL repeatability, customer/site → private proposal → catalogue → sample, authorization/evidence and already identified critical retained financial, stock or scientific defects. The unique blocker register and finite test matrix below govern remaining work. Answered decisions remain binding; justified ordinary defaults are recorded below. |
| 2. Database repeatability | PASS | All final migrations replay successfully in `IsolatedPostgresTestCase` descendants; the named retained ownership/rollback tests pass in the reconciled final checkpoint. PostgreSQL, test database and zero transaction depth are checked before setup writes. Only owned disposable schemas are removed, including the positively identified interrupted-candidate schema; an unrelated older test schema and all existing databases/development records remain untouched. Nonessential migration consolidation is deferred. |
| 3. Bounded core journey | PASS | Every named proposal, intake, numbering, correction and accession test below passes on final source: shared customer/site → private proposal → authorized decision or direct intake, lab/year numbering, atomic invalid-lineage/scope rejection and immutable issued identity. Supporting frontend checks/build pass; authenticated browser/pixel acceptance is not claimed. Full analytical/report-release/amendment expansion remains deferred. |
| 4. Authorization and private files | PASS | Every named direct/bulk/lookup/export/download/queue/switch/revocation test passes. B1, B2 and B4 are closed: private authorized downloads and retained archive/restore, guarded legacy aliases, fail-closed equipment transport/jobs, and active-lab/per-kind export counts. Development privatization verifies zero relevant existing records/public counterparts; isolated fixtures prove old original/conversion bytes are preserved and public copies removed. No known critical exposure is deferred. |
| 5. Transactions, concurrency and evidence | PASS | All finite named numbering, root-rollback, issued-core, payment, reservation, reversal and fresh after-root-commit tests pass. B1's checked file lifecycle and B3's checked atomic stock lifecycle/coordination are closed, including 99 native stock cases and the corrected insufficient-stock error mapping. No hypothetical race audit or new policy is added. |
| 6. Final exact-state verification | PASS | **4,195 passed, two expected skips / 31,599 assertions** in **2,023.80 s**, unchanged launcher and **512 MiB** limit. All **4,197 manifest identities / 196 classes** occur exactly once: no missing, extra, duplicate, failed or errored cases. Before/after **2,943-file** snapshots match, including build assets. Affected frontend **26/26**, production build **22.44 s** (all 631 referenced assets present), Pint and source whitespace checks pass. The generated-bundle literal warning is explained below. F1 documents 15 unrelated visual/localization failures; no assertions were weakened or failing tests skipped. Any later code/build change requires a new checkpoint. |
| 7. Publish to main | PASS | All intended paths were explicitly reviewed/staged, with no secrets/unrelated artifacts. Fresh fetch proved local and remote base `8252289471da96c8e14cc934efaf1eca4209752e` equal (0 ahead/behind), so no merge/rebase was needed. Certified source commit **`745ac4df2862045c9319bcf7d5e3f3ff43f7cc07`** was pushed normally; `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/main` returned exactly that SHA and local HEAD matched. Post-commit source snapshot still matches certification; no hooks altered it. No force push or deployment. This final documentation-only closure is published normally as a follow-up; its local/remote HEAD equality is reported in the completion response. |

No Phase 2 screenshots, redesign, implementation or preparation is started; Claude Code owns it outside Codex. Phase 3 begins only on a later explicit user request. Only this closure bookkeeping follows source publication. |

### Unique remaining blocker register

Each blocker appears once here; other sections are historical evidence, not additional Phase 1 obligations.

| ID / gates | Reproduced evidence | Specific acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| B1 / 4, 5 | Inventory documents defaulted to `public`, with only new tagged uploads guarded; canonical and legacy material/equipment aliases physically deleted media. Unowned generic uploads and user signature/export collections were public; the studio enumerated personal signatures. | `PrivateDocumentAccessTest`, `RetainedDocumentPrivatizationTest`, `ReportStudioWorkflowTest`: protected own-lab/own-kind downloads, retained checked archive/restore including both aliases, rollback/replay and preserved SHA-256 bytes; public-copy removal with verified target/path and checked metadata. Unowned generic documents/exports are administrator quarantine, new studio uploads are private administrator-only images, personal signatures are not a shared gallery. Verified development PostgreSQL cutover finds zero relevant existing records (no existing bytes deleted); isolated fixtures prove original/conversion migration and replay. |
| B2 / 4 | `EquipmentImportController` uploads without `add_iequipments`; `ImportEquipmentsChunk` checks membership but raw-inserts `i_items`, accepts arbitrary category/issued sequence and creates shared related records. `InventoryCatalogueImportTest` proves the canonical action's kind check, but this duplicate writer bypasses it. | Extend `InventoryImportLaboratoryTest` / `InventoryCatalogueImportTest`: equipment permission and fresh membership required, equipment-kind canonical validation, no forged identifiers or partial import/related rows on invalid data. Retire the unsafe duplicate transport if retaining it adds unnecessary scope; any retained queued job must fail closed or use canonical authoring. |
| B3 / 4, 5 | `InventoryController::update()` ignored cancelled model writes; archive/restore used GET and non-atomic per-row writes. The first final candidate also reproduced a missing `ValidationException` import after cleanup (`InventoryLedgerWorkflowTest`, `/private/tmp/lims-phase1-stock-error-red.log`); the failed candidate was interrupted and is **not** completion evidence. | `InventoryPositionMutationTest`, `InventoryTransferConcurrencyTest`: non-GET, exact own-lab/operation permissions, frozen position/history, checked bulk rollback, replay and stock-writer coordination. Restore the exception import and require `InventoryLedgerWorkflowTest` to reject actual insufficient stock (eligible unit fixture), preserve balance, leave zero ledger rows and return the exact `qty` error. Existing adjustment/native files remain required; obtain a new complete checkpoint after this correction. |
| B4 / 4 | `ExportHubTest::test_workspace_counts_use_only_the_active_lab_and_permitted_catalogue_kind` reproduces an HTTP summary returning 2 samples instead of the active lab's 1 (`/private/tmp/lims-frozen-export-reproducer-v4.log`). Three workspace counts use global tables; catalogue visibility also ignores equipment/material permissions. | That named test must return only active-lab, permitted-kind sample/catalogue/maintenance counts; switching and revoked membership must fail closed. Repair only these summary queries, then run the complete `ExportHubTest`. |

### Ordinary decisions and bounded remaining tasks

- Keep answered policies unchanged. Preserve separate equipment permissions, shared customers/private proposals, lab/year identifiers and all retained issued evidence.
- Recommend retiring the raw equipment CSV transport rather than designing a new import subsystem: canonical authoring already exists; the duplicate writer is unsafe and compatibility is not required. Retained records stay unchanged; tests must prove old writes/jobs cannot bypass it.
- Use private, permissioned attachment delivery and non-destructive retained removal for operational documents: public URLs and irreversible deletion undermine already approved privacy/evidence rules. Studio visuals require explicit classification; do not assume a private document is a public brand asset.
- Sequence: intermediate checkpoint reconciled → B1–B4 implemented and affected checks pass → reconcile gates 2–5 on final changes → final complete checkpoint/build/formatting → deliberate main publication and remote SHA proof → hand back and stop. No repeated requests for already authorized full-suite runs or publication.
- Later backlog: exact visual system and pixel matching (Claude Code, Phase 2); broader scientific/report/amendment, supporting-module process redesign, migration consolidation, performance/scalability, external integration crash/idempotency campaigns and unproven hypothetical races. Phase 3 is not started or prepared here.

**Closed in Phase 2 (2026-10-03):** the frontend suite passes 425 / 425 after the contract tests were reconciled with the approved Phase 1 behaviour; see the Phase 2 section. The historical record below is kept as written.

Full frontend run: **410 pass / 15 fail / 425 cases**, `/private/tmp/lims-frozen-front-all.log`. No test or assertion was removed, weakened or skipped. These are design-class/native-control expectations and copy, not failures of authorization, retained bytes, transaction integrity or the bounded customer-to-sample journey. The failing contract files, shared shell, LabNetwork and LaboratoryWorkbench hashes match the earlier intermediate source snapshot; no screenshot/pixel acceptance is claimed. Functional document and stock checks pass separately (26 cases). The existing dossier root styling is not changed by the document lifecycle repair. Retain this issue for Claude Code's Phase 2, without implementing or preparing that phase.

The 14 `design-system-contract.test.mjs` case names: shared form controls expose explicit selection and picker states; analysis tables use semantic selection and data-density controls; shared shell and primitives consume semantic design classes; VAP inventory item dossier uses stock and compliance surfaces; VAP low-stock report uses replenishment assurance surfaces; VAP inventory-value report uses reconciliation and financial-control surfaces; VAP consumption report uses reagent-stewardship and traceability surfaces; VAP stock-movement report uses audit-trail and reconciliation surfaces; VAP inventory reagent consumption uses audit-ready operating surfaces; VAP inventory reagent consumption form and detail share the audit workflow language; commercial document create screens use the compact LIMS form language; sample detail page uses traceability-focused LIMS surfaces; customer portfolio uses a traceability-first directory, dossier, and shared CRM catalogs; inventory delivery create and edit routes share one traceable dispatch form.

The remaining `localization-contract.test.mjs` case is `rendered Vue copy follows the Portuguese localization contract`: four existing “stock” strings in LabNetwork/Index and LaboratoryWorkbench. No critical security or data-integrity defect is waived by F1.

## Outcome and agreed boundaries

Deliver a usable, verified laboratory system with the approved visual direction, PostgreSQL, simpler workflows, and explicit laboratory ownership. Existing code is a starting point, not a requirement to preserve unnecessary steps.

- Customers are shared; proposals and their operational details remain laboratory-private.
- Quotes and import/export certificates are laboratory-owned, including their child records and invoice links, as approved on 2026-10-01. Certificate billing fields are retired from ordinary authoring; only guarded invoice issuance creates the link. Existing invoice links and historical billing states stay preserved.
- Unbilled quotes allow agreed unit prices, as approved on 2026-10-01. The server derives catalog descriptions/tax settings and calculates discounts, tax and totals; client-calculated financial fields are not authoritative.
- Every saved unbilled commercial quote revision receives a new signature and retains prior signatures and snapshots in revision history, as approved on 2026-10-01. Billed quote signatures and commercial content remain locked; ordinary root observations do not change the signature.
- Invoices, credit notes, and receipts are laboratory-owned, as approved on 2026-10-01. Customers remain shared. Required parent/line/allocation ownership and bounded staff-read/payment safeguards are implemented below; source/conversion/archive/concurrency review remains open. Financial network indicators require separate approval.
- A main laboratory may see stock/material availability and approved summary indicators for laboratories in its network. Network membership does not grant access to another laboratory's operational records or permission to change its stock.
- Each integration connector has one owning laboratory; shared connectors require a separate, explicitly approved design. Ownership remains fixed through ordinary edits.
- Branding supports client/network identity and laboratory overrides. Navigation is collapsible. Brand colors must not change the meaning of warning, error, or success states.
- Keep well-maintained Spatie packages. The HighSolutions sequence dependency has already been replaced with an internal allocator; verify its coverage before treating package cleanup as complete.
- Equipment authoring permissions remain separate from material catalogue permissions, as approved on 2026-10-02. Consolidation must preserve equipment-only capabilities without granting material access, losing required fields or bypassing category-aware ownership checks.
- Equipment/material classification uses an explicit category type instead of hard-coded category ID `1`, as approved on 2026-10-03. The category-type foundation and preserving migration are implemented and verified below. Type becomes permanently locked after first use. Canonical interactive authoring preserves separate kind permissions and retires duplicate writers; remaining CSV, lifecycle and read consumers still prevent end-to-end catalogue consolidation.
- Reagent consumption recording requires `add_reagent_consumption`; reversal requires `delete_reagent_consumption`, as approved on 2026-10-02. Reversal retains the original consumption and movement, adds a linked compensating movement and never credits stock twice on replay. Permission registration does not grant account or role access.
- Keep existing MySQL databases and backups untouched. Use the authorized clean PostgreSQL setup; never rebuild the development database to make tests pass.
- This project is preproduction. Remove unused legacy code, duplicate processes, and compatibility-only routes instead of maintaining them by default. Preserve actual current records and evidence; supporting hypothetical legacy deployments is not an acceptance requirement.
- Apply Laravel action classes, Form Requests, policies, API Resources, and Inertia v3 composables where they simplify actual workflows. Do not add abstractions or dependencies merely to use a pattern.
- Sample numbering is separate per laboratory and year, as approved on 2026-09-27. Newly generated identifiers include a stable laboratory token (`SMP-YYYY-L<lab-id>-TYPE-NNNNN`); already issued identifiers are not rewritten. Configurable display formats and numbering for other document types belong to a later, separately verified workflow slice.
- Issued sample/accession identity and analytical scope stay fixed, as approved on 2026-09-28. Ordinary receipt, collection, custody, and descriptive metadata remain editable. A correction is not a way to substitute the customer, site, product, department, source, or issued analytical identifiers.
- Financial documents and quote/import/export billing sources belong to one laboratory, with shared customer/site directories. Invoice links on trade certificates come only from guarded issuance; manual invoice/billed fields are retired. Issued invoice, credit-note and receipt identity, parties, amounts, taxes and lines are locked; ordinary editors correct observations only, as approved on 2026-10-01. Payment and cancellation/correction remain explicit workflows, not general-purpose edits.
- New invoice and receipt signatures include the latest issued predecessor even when archived, as approved on 2026-10-01. Existing signatures are not rewritten; existing invoice year/type and receipt year grouping remain. Unsigned records are not predecessors. Cash-invoice behavior uses category code `FR`, not a numeric category ID.
- Portal surveys require laboratory-issued invitations, as approved on 2026-10-01. Every invitation and response has one owning laboratory and an exact authenticated recipient. Internal staff surveys belong to the active directly joined laboratory; network overview does not grant access to private feedback.
- Notification template presets remain shared; edits become laboratory-owned overrides, as approved on 2026-10-01. A lab administrator must not alter another lab's copy or delivery configuration. Shared preset metadata and destinations are immutable. Only active directly joined staff with settings-edit permission can save or reset an override.
- Manual agreement creation and consent editing are retired, as approved on 2026-10-01. Consent comes through proposal acceptance/rejection; retained agreements, decision logs, and authorized archive/restore remain. Missing historical nondisclosure evidence stays unknown rather than being inferred.
- Duplicate proposal authoring is retired, as approved on 2026-10-01. Older create/edit links redirect to the canonical authoring flow; older POST/PUT writes and their unused forms are removed. Existing records, lists, history, and archive/restore remain.
- Duplicate proposal-template authoring is retired, as approved on 2026-10-01. Older create/edit links redirect to the canonical template editor; unused POST/PUT writes, forms, and request are removed. Shared templates, the retained list, and permissioned archive/restore remain. All archive entry points enforce the canonical used-template protection across laboratories and archived proposal evidence.
- Shared staff accounts: laboratory administrators manage laboratory membership and qualifications only, as approved on 2026-10-01. Account-wide profile/security fields, passwords, global roles/permissions and activation remain with the account owner or system administrator according to capability. Account restrictions, local qualifications and canonical membership add/remove are implemented and verified below; remaining global operation lifecycle/history safeguards remain separate gates.
- Scientific results require insertion → verification → approval, as approved on 2026-10-01. Each stage needs a nonblank value; zero is valid. Ordinary changes cannot replace approved evidence. An amendment workflow requires a separately agreed contract and is not introduced by this approval.

## Evidence baseline

Latest connector-authoring continuation: one transactional `SaveIntegrationConnector` action re-reads and locks the current own-lab connector and selected equipment after the common lab/membership/actor boundary. Request and internal action share validation; the archived-link exception now depends on the locked current FK, not route-model state. Both existing management permissions remain valid, with fresh active/verified direct membership and capability checks before and after writes. Connector, token hash, initial mapping and required audit are checked together; vetoed or altered writes roll back. Complete current-schema root/mapping/audit intent is compared, and no-op root timestamps remain unchanged while existing audit semantics are retained. Final frozen regression passes **125 tests / 978 assertions across six files**, including **37 new integrity/direct cases / 321 assertions** and **12 new native PostgreSQL cases / 150 assertions**. All manifest identities occur exactly once, all 15 relevant file digests remain unchanged, and there are no failures, errors or skips. Pint, whitespace checks and read-only Laravel review pass. No UI, development data/schema, grants, credentials, dependencies or external delivery changed. This closes the tested connector-authoring gap, not every integration writer or the broader catalogue/full-suite/Phase 1 gates.

Latest integration-equipment continuation: hub equipment choices and connector validation now use explicit equipment classification and owning-laboratory scope rather than category ID 1. Manage authorization and update-target laboratory checks run before payload validation. Correct archived equipment links remain readable and visibly selected; other unavailable retained links expose only a boolean and survive unrelated metadata edits without disclosing or resubmitting their item IDs. New material, unclassified, peer-lab and archived assignments are rejected. Final affected regression passes **76 tests / 507 assertions across four files**, including **16 new boundary cases / 125 assertions**, with every manifest identity executed exactly once and no failures, errors or skips. PHP sources and fixtures remained unchanged during that invocation; a subsequent detail-label clarification was separately frontend-tested and rebuilt. Five frontend checks, Pint and read-only Laravel review pass. This is a request/read/form repair, not a locked writer or independent-concurrency guarantee. Browser/pixel acceptance and broader catalogue consolidation remain open; the full-suite checkpoint is still authorized, not newly completed.

Latest consumption-read continuation: register/detail rows and statistics use the owning-laboratory and permitted material/equipment boundary with independent stock-read permission, validated filters and bounded pages. Explicit resources retain archived item, category, unit, warehouse, supplier and operator identification without full profiles or costs. Dashboard stock alerts follow the same read boundary. Intake defaults to the signed-in operator, preserves entered technician text, follows the server's authorized save destination and offers a safe back/cancel destination. Archived items show an identification notice instead of a dead active-only link. Final frozen regression passes **317 tests / 2,401 assertions across 18 files**, including **20 new read cases / 233 assertions**, with zero failures, errors or skips; every manifest identity occurs exactly once and all 31 relevant source/test digests remain unchanged. Sixteen frontend checks, production build, Pint and bounded read-only Laravel review pass. Better UI and Emil guided these functional affordances, not a layout redesign. Browser/pixel acceptance remains deferred. Creation deliberately retains existing add-only access with minimal own-lab choices while its separately requested read-permission policy remains unanswered. No development data/schema, accounts, credentials, permission grants, dependencies or external delivery changed. Broader catalogue consolidation, its authorized full-suite rerun and the Phase 1 exit audit remain unfinished.

Previous operational-report continuation: stock movement, consumption, valuation, report downloads and the legacy ledger register now use one owning-laboratory and permitted material/equipment query boundary. Rows, aggregates, charts and validated filters agree; retained labels remain readable without exposing full user profiles or appended signature URLs. Daily averages use the same filtered data and inclusive selected/observed dates. CSV text formulas are neutralized without changing signed four-decimal quantities, and low-stock download severity is respected. Final post-Pint regression passes **159 tests / 1,617 assertions across 12 files**, including **60 new cases / 500 assertions**, with no failures, errors or skips. All manifest identities occur exactly once and the 19 relevant source/test digests remain unchanged. Earlier Phase 1 slice: public proposal acceptance/rejection now uses one validated transactional action. It locks and reloads the proposal by its original public token, checks the current owning lab/customer/site/owner snapshot, and applies the existing SENT/VIEWED/REVISED eligibility rules before evidence writes. Concurrent replies cannot overwrite a completed decision; later replies retain the existing HTTP 400 response. Token rotation, source archival, reassignment, and invalid customer/site lineage fail closed. Agreement, acceptance-log, proposal, and audit saves are checked; vetoes roll back all decision evidence. Multiple active agreements are not silently overwritten. Public page-view tracking uses the same proposal-row serialization, so a stale read cannot replace a terminal decision with VIEWED or repeat view audit entries. Notifications wait for the real root commit, recheck source/token/current decision, and report delivery/enqueue exceptions independently of the recorded decision response. Existing consent values (including false), rejection reasons, redirects, and request validation remain; no new scientific approval, expiry, amendment, or mandatory-positive-consent rule was inferred. Verification is recorded under the current requirement audit below. The full PHP suite remains deferred to the next broader checkpoint, as requested on 2026-10-01. No frontend, dependencies, development records, or schema changed in this slice.
